Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the stochastic framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 118. The resulting operator achieves bound O(81/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_118(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 118. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^81(S, F) is trivial. QED.
